Pseudo:
```
def improvement_search(start, time_budget, n? ):
   a = start copy, ensure nonneg, sum=1.
   best = a, best_score=fast_score.
   for iteration while time:
      c = conv_fft(a,a)
      d = n*c  (scaled)
      maxd = max(d); meand = ~0.5.
      scale = max(1e-6, max(meand*0.2, (maxd - meand)*0.1)) # temperature
      w = exp((d - maxd)/scale)  # weights sum peak 1, others small; maybe add baseline 1e-3 to avoid only max
      w /= sum(w)
      # gradient q_i = n * conv(a_rev, w)[n-1:n-1+n] (maybe times 2; step absorbs)
      q = conv_fft(a[::-1], w)[n-1:n-1+n] * n  # maybe no 2.
      # normalize q relative? mean_q = mean(q) maybe ~? d? Keep.
      for eta in [0.001, 0.003,0.01, 0.03,0.1,0.3,1.0,3.0]:
          b = a * exp(-eta * q)
          b = np.maximum(b, eps)
          b /= b.sum()
          s = fast_score(b)
          if s < best_local: accept
      if no improvement: adjust scale (increase temp), or use random perturb.
   exact refine maybe
```

```
def param_objective(p):
   t = grid # [0,1]
   z = p[0] + p[1]*t + p[2]*t**2 + ... maybe plus sine? 
   a = np.exp(np.clip(z, -50, 50)) # or softplus? exp easier
   # Need positivity not too huge; if not finite return inf
   a[s]<maxa? no
   a /= a.sum()
   c = convolve_fft(a,a)
   d = n*c
   # return logsumexp(β*(d - shift)) + perhaps penalty for max endpoint?
```

Convolution via rfft:
```
def convolution_fft(a, b=None):
    a=np.asarray(a,float); if b None b=a
    n=len(a); m=len(b)
    L=1
    while L < n+m-1: L <<=1
    fa=np.zeros(L, complex); fa[:n]=a; fa=np.fft.rfft(fa)
    if b is None: fb=fa
    else: fb=np.fft.rfft(zeros)
    out=np.fft.irfft(fa*fb, L)[:n+m-1]
    out[np.abs(out)<1e-15]=0
    return out
```

```
def lfgs_smooth(x, n, beta):
   x0 = np.concatenate(([0.0], x)) # x free length n-1
   z = np.exp(np.clip(x0, -50, 50))
   a = z / z.sum()
   c = convolve_self(a)
   d = n*c
   # stable logsumexp: m=max(d); val=np.log(np.sum(np.exp(beta*(d-m)))) + beta*m
   # weights p = exp(beta*(d-m))/sum
   # gradA_i = 2*n*conv(a[::-1], p)[n-1:n-1+n]  # p sum? p currently normalized? p=exp/Z, sum1.
   s = np.sum(a*gradA)
   grad = np.zeros(n-1); grad = a[1:]*gradA[1:] - a[1:]*s  # but missing -a0*s? Since sum includes all: a_j gA_j - a_j*s. This is correct for free x_j.
   # If some a too small, gradient fine. Return val, grad
```

Convolution:
```
def convolve_fft(a, b=None):
    a=np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if b is None:
        n=a.size
        L=sfft.next_fast_len(2*n-1)
        fa=sfft.rfft(a, n=L)
        y=sfft.irfft(fa*fa, n=L)
        return y[:2*n-1].astype(np.float64)
    else: b... lengths n,m, L=next_fast_len(n+m-1)
```
Need ensure no small negative due FFT; clip where `y<0 and >-1e-12`? For score max, if negative small, not issue. For gradients, negative w? Use `np.maximum(y,0)` for conv of nonnegative maybe but could clip true small negative? fine. Use if needed.

For score, max of y. If `mmax<0` set 0. Use S=sum.

Maybe use real FFT `rfft` with input zeros; for self conv can use `sfft.rfft(a,n=L), out=sfft.irfft(fa*fa,n=L)`.

### Score fast
```
def fast_score(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size==0 or not np.isfinite(a).all(): return inf
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    S=a.sum()
    if S<1e-12: return inf
    c=convolve_fft(a)
    m=max(c)
    return float(2.0*a.size*max(m,0.0)/(S*S))
```
FFT approximate; for exact final use evaluate_sequence.

### Normalization
```
def normalize_sum(a, target=1.0):
   a=np.maximum(a,0); S=a.sum(); if S<=0 return zeros+?; return a/S*target
```

### Subgradient/local solver
Implement as top-level.
```
def subgradient_refine(a, max_time, verbose=False):
    start_time=time.monotonic()
    a=normalize_sum(a)
    n=a.size
    best=a.copy(); best_score=fast_score(a)
    # hyperparams: temp_factor 0.15->0.8, step candidates
    it=0
    stall=0
    temp_factor=0.2
    alpha_prof=0
    while time.monotonic()-start_time < max_time and best_score>target? maybe continue:
       c=convolve_fft(a)
       d=n*c
       maxd=np.max(d)
       # If maxd insufficient maybe
       med=float(np.median(d))
       if maxd - med < 1e-8:
           temp=1e-3
       else:
           temp=(maxd-med)*temp_factor
       z=d - maxd
       # Use top L to avoid underflow: if temp small, set z min -700
       z=np.clip(z/temp, -700, 0)
       w=np.exp(z)
       w += 1e-6
       w /= w.sum()
       # Gradient
       q=convolve_fft(a[::-1], w)[n-1:n-1+n]
       q = q * n
       q = np.maximum(q, 0) # nonnegative? Conv nonneg. small neg clip.
       # normalize q scale
       qs = np.percentile(q, 95)
       if qs <= 1e-12: qs=1
       qn = np.clip(q/qs, -10, 10)
       # line search
       improved=False
       # candidate etas dynamic based on previous? list
       etas = [0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.5,1.0,2.0,5.0,10.0]
       best_step_score=best_score
       for eta in etas:
           b=a*np.exp(-eta*qn)
           # floor to prevent zeros: b = np.maximum(b, 1e-300)
           b=np.maximum(b, 1e-300)
           S=b.sum()
           if S<=0: continue
           b /= S
           sc=fast_score(b)
           if sc < best_step_score:
               best_step_score=sc; best_step=b.copy(); chosen_eta=eta
       if improved:
           a=best_step
           if sc<best_score: update global best
           stall=0
           # decrease temp_factor (focus) slightly
           temp_factor=max(0.05, temp_factor*0.98)
       else:
           stall +=1
           # change hyperparams: increase temp, random perturb
           temp_factor=min(1.0, temp_factor*1.5)
           # random perturbation
           if stall % 5 == 0:
               noise = np.exp(rng.normal(0, 0.05, n)) # but rng top-level? Generate from global? Use np.random.default_rng with seed maybe.
               b=a*noise; b=normalize_sum(b); sc=fast_score(b)
               if sc < best_score*1.05 and sc < best_step? maybe if not too bad, accept
           if stall>30: break
    return best
```
Need rng. Use deterministic local rng from seed; pass or global. We can create `rng = np.random.default_rng(123)`.

This subgradient may be slow if line search 9 FFTs per iteration. For n=4096, one score ~2 FFT? fast_score only self conv (1 rfft + irfft). q convolve another. So each iter ~1+self? For 9 candidates ~10 conv ~0.5M, fine. 1000 iter ~500M maybe seconds.

Could line search use only 4 etas. And accept best local even if not global to maintain. But if `best_step_score` not < `best_score`, but < current score, we can still accept? We want progress. If current score > best_score due earlier? We always set a=best? In loop a should be best. If no candidate improves current, try random. If a may be best, yes. If a current score maybe best. Use line search to improve current. If no, change.

Maybe use exact score occasionally to track: If fast_score ~ best, call exact maybe every 5000 eval? Too much. Final enough.

Need target: We can stop subgradient if best_score < 1.5027? But lower still good. Continue until time.

Potential problem: multiplicative update with q gradient decreases entire distribution but normalization; q distribution might be dominated by endpoint if active lag near 0? It can converge to zeros? Floor prevents.

### L-BFGS smooth solver
Implement as top-level. Need use `scipy.optimize.minimize`. We need not use closures, but `fun` can be a method with params? To pass n, beta, free mapping without closure, define a class? Helpers top-level with global? Or `fun` defined top-level taking `x, state` and `scipy` calls `fun(x)` only; we can use `functools.partial`? No closures? Lambdas/closures prohibited; partial maybe not closure but from function nesting? Better define small state in global variables? But each solver run can set module-level globals? That's okay?
```
start = time.monotonic()
while time.monotonic() - start < time_budget:
   it +=1
   c=convolve_fft(a)
   d=n*c
   maxd=float(np.max(d))
   if maxd < 1e-6: break
   mean=float(np.mean(d)) # ~0.5
   temp_factor = start_temp_factor + (end-start)*min(1, it/100) maybe
   temp=(maxd-mean)*temp_factor
   if temp<1e-6: temp=1e-3
   z=(d-maxd)/temp
   w=np.exp(np.clip(z,-700,0))
   w[isfinite]+=1e-5? add 1e-6
   w=w/w.sum()
   q=convolve_fft(a[::-1], w)
   q=q[n-1:n-1+n]
   q=q*n
   q=np.maximum(q,0)
   # normalize
   p95=np.percentile(q,95) if q.size else 1
   if p95<1e-8: p95=1
   qn=np.clip(q/p95, -20,20)
   # etas: if current_score near target? smaller
   etas = [0.01,0.03,0.1,0.3,1.0,3.0]
   best_local=a; best_local_score=current_score; chosen=False
   for eta in etas:
       candidate = a*np.exp(-eta*qn)
       candidate=np.maximum(candidate, 1e-300)
       S=candidate.sum()
       if S<=0: continue
       candidate/=S
       sc=fast_score(candidate)
       if sc < best_local_score - 1e-12:
           best_local_score=sc; best_local=candidate; chosen=True
   if chosen:
       a=best_local; current_score=best_local_score
       add local to history? At end return best.
       temp_factor *= 0.985
       stall=0
   else:
       stall+=1
       temp_factor = min(1.5, temp_factor*1.4)
       if stall >=3:
           # random perturb based on noise level decreasing/increasing
           noise_level = 0.05 + 0.05*math.sin(stall/5)
           vec = np.exp(rng.normal(0, noise_level, n))
           candidate=a*vec; candidate=normalize_sum(candidate); sc=fast_score(candidate)
           if sc < current_score * 1.1 and sc < best_seen*1.02:
                a=candidate; current_score=sc; stall=0
           elif stall > 20:
                break
```

```
def run_lbfgs(a, max_time, betas=(3,8,20), maxiter=120, maxfun=300):
   a=normalize_sum(a); n=a.size
   ref=int(np.argmax(a))
   free_mask=np.ones(n,bool); free_mask[ref]=False; freeidx=np.nonzero(free_mask)[0]
   # initial x = log(a/max(a_ref))
   x0=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300)/max(a[ref],1e-300)); x0[ref]=0; x0=clip(x0, -40,40); x= x0[freeidx]
   best=a; best_score=fast_score(a)
   for beta in betas:
       obj=_SmoothObjective(n,beta,freeidx,ref)
       x_init=x.copy()
       try:
          res=sopt.minimize(obj, x_init, method='L-BFGS-B', options={'maxiter':maxiter, 'maxfun':maxfun, 'ftol':1e-10, 'gtol':1e-7, 'disp':False})
       except Exception: ...
       x_init=res.x if finite else x_init
       # reconstruct a from x
       x_full=zeros; ...; a_new=normalize(exp(clip))
       sc=fast_score(a_new)
       if sc<best_score: best=a_new; best_score=sc; x=x_init
       if time remaining <... break
   return best
```

### Starting list and loop
Let's define helper `make_starts(n, parent_info, rng)`.
```
def make_starts(n, parent, rng):
   starts=[]
   starts.append(np.ones(n)/n)
   for alpha in [-6,-3,-1.5,1.5,3,6]:
      x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
      a=np.exp(alpha*(x-0.5)) # nonneg; normalize
      starts.append(normalize(a))
   for p in [0.5,1,2,4]:
      x=np.linspace(0,1,n); a=(1-x)**p + 1e-6; starts.append(normalize(a))
      a=(x)**p + 1e-6; starts.append(normalize(a))
   # random smooth starts
   for _ in range(8):
      r=rng.normal(size=n)
      for _ in range(2): r=np.cumsum(r); r -= r[0] - (r[-1]-r[0])? # remove drift? 
      a=np.exp(r*0.5); starts.append(normalize(a))
   # random polynomial exp degree 5
   for _ in range(10):
      coeffs=rng.normal(size=6)*1.5
      x=np.linspace(0,1,n); t=x-0.5
      z=0
      power=np.ones(n)
      for c in coeffs: z += c*power; power*=t
      a=np.exp(np.clip(z,-10,10)); starts.append(normalize(a))
   # parent interpolate
   if parent is not None and len(parent)>0:
      p=parent; if len(p)>2? a=interpolate; starts.append(normalize(a)); starts.append(normalize(a[::-1]))
   return starts
```

```
def run_lp_refine(a, max_time):
    t0=time.monotonic(); a=normalize_sum(a); n=a.size
    if n>512: return a
    best=a; best_score=fast_score(a)
    for it in range(50):
       c=convolve_fft(a)
       M_max=float(np.max(c))
       if M_max <=0: break
       # maybe scale to avoid tiny? M= M_max*(1+1e-8)
       A = convolution_matrix(a) # shape (2n-1,n)
       try:
          res=sopt.linprog(c=-np.ones(n), A_ub=A, b_ub=np.full(2n-1, M_max*1.0001), bounds=[(0,None)]*n, method='highs')
       except: break
       if not res.success or not finite: break
       g=res.x
       if g is None: break
       g=np.maximum(g,0); Sg=g.sum()
       if Sg <= 0: break
       g /= Sg
       # line search
       improved=False
       for t in [0.1,0.25,0.5,0.75,1.0]:
           h=(1-t)*a + t*g; h=normalize_sum(h)
           sc=fast_score(h)
           if sc < best_score: update
       if no improved and it>10: break
       else a=best? If best updated, set a=bestseq
       if time > break
    return best
```

### More robust LP matrix construction
For n ≤512 dense. But if f sum1, M maybe 1e-4. Linprog with b_ub small and objective maximize sum; f feasible. Good.
```
def build_conv_matrix(f):
   n=f.size; m=2*n-1; A=np.zeros((m,n), dtype=np.float64)
   # for each j, k=j..j+n-1? coefficient f[k-j]
   for j in range(n):
       # k valid from j to j+n-1
       # f indices k-j from 0..n-1
       A[j:j+n, j] = f
   return A
```
This O(n^2). Good.

LP solve bounds: use list? `bounds=[(0.0, None)]*n` maybe okay. HiGHS accepts. Could be slower. Use `bounds=np.zeros((n,2)); bounds[:,1]=np.inf`. Better.

### Exact polish function
```
def exact_local_refine(seq, time_budget, rng, max_evals=200):
    start=time.monotonic()
    seq=normalize_sum(seq)
    score=exact_score(seq)
    if not finite: return seq,score
    n=seq.size
    for i in range(max_evals):
        if time.monotonic()-start > time_budget: break
        # random block perturb
        width = int(n * (0.005 + rng.random()*0.05)) if n large else 1
        width = max(1, min(n, width))
        start_idx = int(rng.integers(0, n-width+1)) if n>width else 0
        b=seq.copy()
        b[start_idx:start_idx+width] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.15))
        b=normalize_sum(b)
        sc=exact_score(b)
        if sc < score - 1e-12:
            seq=b; score=sc
    return seq,score
```
Exact eval count limited by time. For n=6733, each exact maybe 0.1s, 200=20s. Good.

### Normalizing and clipping
`normalize_sum` should allocate. If S nan return zeros? Ensure finite:
```
def sanitize_sequence(a):
   a=np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
   a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
   a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
   a=np.minimum(a,1000.0)
   return a
```

### Start generation details no closures
```
def make_starts(n, parent, rng, max_starts=80):
    starts=[]
    x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    # uniform
    starts.append(np.ones(n,n?)/n)
    # exponentials
    for alpha in (-8.0,-5.0,-3.0,-1.0,1.0,3.0,5.0,8.0):
        z=np.clip(alpha*x, -30,30)
        a=np.exp(z); starts.append(normalize_sum(a))
    # powers
    for p in (0.5,1.0,2.0,4.0):
        a=np.power(1.0 - 0.98*x, p); starts.append(normalize_sum(a+1e-6))
        a=np.power(0.02 + x, p); starts.append(normalize_sum(a+1e-6))
    # asymmetric polynomials
    for p in (2,3,4,5):
       a=np.power(1.0 + 0.5*(x-0.5) + 0.5*((x-0.5)**2), 0?) maybe not.
    # random smooth
    for _ in range(12): ...
    # parent interpolations
```

Random smooth: `r = rng.standard_normal(n)`, apply cumsum twice, subtract linear trend to remove drift? Could cause negative after exp? exp positive. Normalize.
```
for _ in range(12):
   r = rng.standard_normal(n)
   r = np.cumsum(r)
   r = r - np.linspace(r[0], r[-1], n)
   r = np.cumsum(r)
   r = r - np.linspace(r[0], r[-1], n)
   a=np.exp(np.clip(r*0.5, -20,20))
   starts.append(normalize_sum(a))
```

```
for _ in range(16):
   deg=rng.integers(3,9)
   coeff=rng.normal(size=int(deg+1))*1.2
   t=x-0.5
   z=np.zeros(n)
   pow=np.ones(n)
   for c in coeff:
      z += c*pow; pow=pow*t
   a=np.exp(np.clip(z,-12,12)); starts.append(normalize_sum(a))
```

### run_refine implementation details
Need no closures. Use helper `run_lbfgs` and `run_subgradient`.
```
def run_refine(a, time_budget, rng, allow_lbfgs=True):
    t0=time.monotonic(); a=sanitize_sequence(a); best=normalize_sum(a); best_score=fast_score(best); current=best.copy()
    if best_score==inf: return best
    lbfgs_budget = min(180.0, 0.35*time_budget) if allow_lbfgs and time_budget>60 else 0.0
    if lbfgs_budget > 20:
       a_l=run_lbfgs(current, lbfgs_budget, betas=(3.0,6.0,12.0))
       sc=fast_score(a_l)
       if sc < best_score: best=a_l; best_score=sc
       # set current to best for subgradient? Maybe if a_l worse but has different shape, use current = a_l if sc < current_score*1.05? But to progress, use best.
       current = best if current=best else current? Let's set current = a_l if sc < current_score + 0.05? Could explore local of L-BFGS even if slightly worse, subgradient may fix. But if L-BFGS worsened a lot, don't.
       if sc < best_score + 0.2:
          current = a_l
    sub_budget = time_budget - (time.monotonic()-t0) - 1.0
    if sub_budget > 10:
       a_s=run_subgradient(current, sub_budget, rng)
       sc=fast_score(a_s)
       if sc < best_score: best=a_s; best_score=sc
    return best
```
Subgradient itself tracks best from its start; if current worse than best, it may return best if includes? It starts from current and best=current, not global. If current worse, may not recover to old best. But we use current=best if not. Fine.

### run_subgradient line search best selection
Use `fast_score` many times. We can optimize by checking FFT score; if candidate nonfinite skip. Track best_seen.
If improvement relative to current but not best_seen, still update a to candidate? To gradually move; if best_seen remains, at end return best_seen. But if we update to candidate worse than best, later may improve. If candidate not < current, no move. So do:
```
current_score = fast_score(a)
best=a; best_score=current_score
while ...:
   ... line search returns best_step_score (may be less than current_score)
   if best_step_score < current_score:
       a=best_step; current_score=best_step

   if no improvement: random perturb maybe accept if < current_score*1.2 (even if >best) to escape? If accept worse than best, current moves, but best kept.
```
If random accept worse than current but not too, can escape local. But if current is already best, random worse may not lead back. We can maintain `best` and return best.

```
    return [float(float(x)) for x in final]
```

#### Imports
```
import time
import math
import operator

import scipy.fft as sfft
```

#### sanitize
```
def sanitize_sequence(a):
    if a is None: return np.empty(0,dtype=float)
    out=np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if out.size==0: return out
    out=np.where(np.isfinite(out), out, 0.0)
    out=np.maximum(out, 0.0)
    out=np.minimum(out, 1000.0)
    return out
```

#### normalize_sum
```
def normalize_sequence(a, target=1.0):
    a=sanitize_sequence(a)
    if a.size==0: return a
    s=float(np.sum(a))
    if s <= 0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64)* (target / a.size)
    return a / s * target
```
If all zeros, uniform.

#### convolve_fft
Need handle length 1. For length 1, output [a0*a1?]. rfft works.
```
def convolve_fft(a, b=None):
    a=sanitize_sequence(a)
    if a.size==0: return np.array([], dtype=float)
    if b is None:
        n=a.size; L=int(sfft.next_fast_len(2*n-1))
        fa=sfft.rfft(a, n=L)
        y=sfft.irfft(fa*fa, n=L)
        out=y[:2*n-1]
    else:
        b=sanitize_sequence(b)
        n=a.size; m=b.size; if m==0: return zeros?
        L=int(sfft.next_fast_len(n+m-1))
        fa=sfft.rfft(a,n=L); fb=sfft.rfft(b,n=L)
        y=sfft.irfft(fa*fb,n=L); out=y[:n+m-1]
    out=np.asarray(out,dtype=np.float64)
    out=np.where(out < 0.0, 0.0, out)
    return out
```
If b sequence is weights length 2n-1 with sum1, a length n; L next_fast_len(3n-3). Good.

Potential memory: irfft returns length L, slice. We allocate out. Fine.

#### fast_score
```
def fast_score(a):
   a=sanitize_sequence(a)
   n=a.size
   if n==0: return inf
   s=sum(a)
   if s<1e-12: return inf
   c=convolve_fft(a)
   m=float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
   if not finite: return inf
   return 2.0*n*m/(s*s)
```
If m negative, zero. Good.

#### exact_score_array
```
def exact_score_array(a):
   a=sanitize_sequence(a)
   if a.size==0: return inf
   if a.size > 12000:
      return fast_score(a)
   try:
      val=evaluate_sequence([float(v) for v in a])
   except Exception:
      val=float('inf')
   if not math.isfinite(val): return float('inf')
   return float(val)
```

#### add_history
```
def add_history(history, a, score=None, max_items=24):
    a=sanitize_sequence(a)
    if a.size==0: return
    if score is None or not math.isfinite(float(score)):
        score=fast_score(a)
    else:
        score=float(score)
    if not math.isfinite(score): return
    arr=a.astype(np.float64, copy=True)
    history.append((score, arr))
    history.sort(key=operator.itemgetter(0))
    if len(history) > max_items:
        del history[-1]
```
If we add with exact score not fast, sorting wrong. We'll pass fast score. For parent exact, also compute fast.

#### interpolate_sequence
```
def interpolate_sequence(a, n):
   a=normalize_sequence(a)
   n=int(max(1,n))
   if a.size==0: return np.ones(n)/n
   if a.size==1: return np.full(n, a[0]*a.size/n? raw? Actually normalized sum; return full(n, target/n). Since target=1: np.full(n,1/n)
   x_old=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,a.size)
   x_new=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
   b=np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
   return normalize_sequence(b)
```

#### rank_starts
```
def rank_starts(starts, top_k=10):
    ranked=[]
    for a in starts:
       a=normalize_sequence(a)
       sc=fast_score(a)
       if math.isfinite(sc): ranked.append((sc,a))
    ranked.sort(key=operator.itemgetter(0))
    if len(ranked)>top_k: ranked=ranked[:top_k]
    return ranked
```
If too many, top.

#### run_lbfgs class
Class top-level. Need store `free_idx`, `ref`, `n`, `beta`. Use `self`.
```
class _SmoothObjective:
    def __init__(self, n, beta, free_idx, ref): ...
    def __call__(self, x):
       n=self.n; free_idx=self.free_idx; ref=self.ref; beta=self.beta
       x=np.asarray(x, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
       if x.size != free_idx.size: return 1e100, np.zeros(free_idx.size)
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)): return 1e100, zeros
       x_full=np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
       x_full[free_idx]=x
       # ref zero (already)
       z=np.clip(x_full, -25.0, 25.0)
       a=np.exp(z)
       s=a.sum()
       if s<=0 or not finite: return 1e100, zeros
       a=a/s
       c=convolve_fft(a)
       d=n*c
       if d.size==0: return 1e100, zeros
       m=float(np.max(d))
       zscore=beta*(d-m)
       zscore=np.clip(zscore, -700.0, 0.0)
       e=np.exp(zscore)
       sume=float(np.sum(e))
       if sume <=0 or not finite: return 1e100, zeros
       p=e/sume
       val=beta*m + math.log(sume)
       gvec=convolve_fft(a[::-1], p)
       if gvec.size < 3*n-2? Actually length n + (2n-1) -1 = 3n-2? Check: a length n (reversed), p length 2n-1, sum-1 = 3n-3? If n=2, length 2+3-1=4=3n-2? Wait p length for p is 2n-2? For convolution a*p where a length n, p length 2n-1, full length n+(2n-1)-1=3n-2? Example n=3: a len3, p len5 => full len7 =3n-2=7. The earlier I said 3n-3; off by one? 2n-1 p length, a n => 3n-2. We need slice indices n-1 to n-1+n inclusive length n: positions n-1 .. 2n-2 (inclusive). For n=3 positions2..4. OK.
       grad_full = gvec[n-1:n-1+n] # length n if enough
       if grad_full.size != n: pad? If smaller return inf.
       gA=(2.0*beta*n)*grad_full
       # gA could have tiny negative; but p,a nonnegative, clip.
       gA=np.maximum(gA,0.0) # Is gradient can be negative? No coefficients positive, conv nonnegative. So okay.
       sprod=float(np.dot(a,gA))
       grad = a[free_idx]*(gA[free_idx] - sprod)
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)): return 1e100, zeros
       return float(val), grad
```
Check gvec length: if n=1, p length1, a length1, full len1, slice n-1=0 to1 OK. gvec size 1. 3n-2=1. Good.

Use `math.log` requires sume >0.

In `run_lbfgs`, need `sopt.minimize`. It might pass x as list; fine. Set `tol`? Use options.
```
def run_lbfgs(a, time_budget, betas=(3.0,6.0,12.0)):
   t0=time.monotonic(); a=normalize_sequence(a); n=a.size
   if n<2 or time_budget<20: return a
   ref=int(np.argmax(a))
   free_mask=np.ones(n, dtype=bool); free_mask[ref]=False
   free_idx=np.nonzero(free_mask)[0]
   a_ref=max(float(a[ref]),1e-300)
   x_full=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300)/a_ref); x_full[ref]=0; x_full=np.clip(x_full,-25,25)
   x=x_full[free_idx].copy()
   best=a.copy(); best_score=fast_score(a)
   if n <= 1024: maxfun=350; maxiter=150
   elif n <= 2048: maxfun=250; maxiter=100
   elif n <= 4096: maxfun=180; maxiter=80
   else: maxfun=100; maxiter=50
   for beta in betas:
      if time.monotonic()-t0 >= time_budget - 1: break
      obj=_SmoothObjective(n, float(beta), free_idx, ref)
      try:
         x=None
         # Maybe test first function? minimize.
         res=sopt.minimize(obj, x.copy(), method='L-BFGS-B', options={'maxiter':maxiter, 'maxfun':maxfun, 'ftol':1e-12, 'gtol':1e-8, 'disp':False})
         if res.x is not None and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)): x=res.x.astype(float)
      except Exception:
         pass
      if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)) or x.size != free_idx.size: break? continue
      # reconstruct
      x_full=np.zeros(n); x_full[free_idx]=x; x_full[ref]=0
      z=np.clip(x_full,-25,25); arr=np.exp(z); S=arr.sum(); if S>0: arr=arr/S
      sc=fast_score(arr)
      if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
          best=arr; best_score=sc
      # else maybe if close keep x? no.
   return best
```
If `minimize` takes long and we don't pause, time budget may exceed. Could set time-based maxfun: estimate time per eval? We can run first beta and measure? Av. Simpler use maxfun enough. But if 3 betas each maybe 5s, fine. For n=4096 maybe 30s each. Budget 180. Good.

Could add `if time.monotonic() - t0 > time_budget` after minimize no control. Hard. Use conservative maxfun. 100 evals for n=4096 maybe ~5-10s. 3 betas 30s. OK.

#### run_subgradient
Need use rng. Could accept `min_time`? Use `max_time`.
```
def run_subgradient(a, max_time, rng):
   t0=time.monotonic(); a=normalize_sequence(a); n=a.size
   if n==0 or time_budget<=0: return a
   best=a.copy(); best_score=fast_score(a); current=a.copy(); current_score=best_score
   temp_factor=0.25
   stall=0
   it=0
   while time.monotonic()-t0 < max_time - 0.5 and math.isfinite(current_score):
       it+=1
       c=convolve_fft(current)
       d=n*c
       if d.size==0: break
       maxd=float(np.max(d)); mean=float(np.mean(d))
       if maxd <= mean + 1e-12: break
       temp=(maxd-mean)*temp_factor
       if temp < 1e-4: temp=1e-3
       z=(d-maxd)/temp
       z=np.clip(z,-700,0)
       w=np.exp(z); w += 1e-7; wsum=w.sum(); if wsum<=0: break; w/=wsum
       g=convolve_fft(current[::-1], w)
       if g.size < 2*n-1? len 3n-2. Need if g.size < n-1+n: break
       q=g[n-1:n-1+n]
       if q.size != n: break
       q=np.maximum(q,0.0)
       q*=n
       # scale
       p95=float(np.percentile(q,95)) if n>0 else 1.0
       if p95 < 1e-12: p95=1.0
       qn=np.clip(q/p95, -10.0,10.0)
       etas=(0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.5,1.0,2.0,4.0)
       step_best_score=current_score
       step_best=current
       improved=False
       for eta in etas:
           cand=current*np.exp(-eta*qn)
           cand=np.maximum(cand,1e-300)
           S=float(np.sum(cand))
           if S<=0 or not finite: continue
           cand=cand/S
           sc=fast_score(cand)
           if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < step_best_score - 1e-12:
               step_best_score=sc; step_best=cand; improved=True
       if improved:
           current=step_best; current_score=step_best_score
           if current_score < best_score:
               best=current; best_score=current_score
           stall=0
           temp_factor=max(0.05, temp_factor*0.985)
       else:
           stall += 1
           temp_factor=min(1.2, temp_factor*1.5)
           if stall == 3 or stall % 7 == 0:
               # random perturbation from best maybe
               noise_level = 0.05 + 0.03*math.sin(0.7*stall)
               vec=np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, noise_level, size=n))
               cand=best*vec if stall >10 else current*vec? To not drift too far, start from best when stall high.
               cand=normalize_sequence(cand)
               sc=fast_score(cand)
               if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score*1.02 and sc < current_score*1.5:
                   current=cand; current_score=sc
                   # do not update best if worse
               if stall > 25:
                   break
   return best
```
If random from best and sc slightly worse than best, current not best; but best kept. If stall>10, maybe reset current=best each random. Simpler: if stall >=5, `base=best` else `base=current`. Accept if sc < best_score*1.03 and sc < base_score*1.5. Also if sc < best_score, update best.

Potential issue: If no improvement but stall resets temp high, the algorithm may not progress. Good.

#### run_lp_refine
```
def run_lp_refine(a, max_time):
   t0=time.monotonic(); a=normalize_sequence(a); n=a.size
   if n<8 or n>512 or max_time<5: return a
   best=a.copy(); best_score=fast_score(a)
   c=-np.ones(n)
   bounds=np.zeros((n,2)); bounds[:,1]=np.inf
   for it in range(35):
       if time.monotonic()-t0 > max_time - 1: break
       conv=convolve_fft(best)
       M=float(np.max(conv))
       if not finite or M<=0: break
       # build A
       A=np.zeros((2*n-1,n), dtype=float)
       for j in range(n):
           A[j:j+n,j]=best
       b_ub=np.full(2*n-1, M*(1.0+1e-8))
       try:
          res=sopt.linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=bounds, method='highs')
       except Exception:
          break
       if res is None or not res.success or res.x is None: break
       g=np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float)
       g=sanitize_sequence(g); Sg=float(np.sum(g))
       if Sg<=0 or not finite: break
       g/=Sg
       improved=False
       for t in (0.1,0.25,0.5,0.75,1.0):
          h=(1.0-t)*best + t*g
          h=normalize_sequence(h)
          sc=fast_score(h)
          if finite and sc < best_score - 1e-10:
             best=h; best_score=sc; improved=True
       if not improved:
          # maybe accept g if better than current best? it's not.
          if iter? break after few
          stall +=1; if stall>6: break
       else: stall=0
   return best
```
This uses `best` as current f; if not improved, f unchanged. LP with same f gives same g; stall break. Good.

Could building A with Python loop O(n^2) for n=512, 262k assignments; fine. For n=256 fast.

Potential issue: `linprog` returns `res.x` feasible for max sum. But if there are multiple extreme, hiGHS may choose something. Good.

#### exact_local_refine

```
def exact_local_refine(a, max_time, rng):
   t0=time.monotonic(); a=normalize_sequence(a); n=a.size
   score=exact_score_array(a)
   if not finite: return a
   best=a.copy(); best_score=score
   for _ in range(2000):
      if time.monotonic()-t0 > max_time - 2: break
      width = max(1, int(n * (0.003 + 0.04*rng.random())))
      if width >= n: width=max(1,n//4)
      start=int(rng.integers(0, n-width+1)) if n>width else 0
      b=best.copy()
      b[start:start+width] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.08 + 0.1*rng.random()))
      b=normalize_sequence(b)
      sc=exact_score_array(b)
      if finite and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
         best=b; best_score=sc
   return best
```
If exact_score_array returns fast for large, okay.

#### choose final n
```
def choose_final_n(parent, current_best_size, time_left):
   if parent is not None and 4096 <= parent.size <= 8192:
       return parent.size
   if current_best_size >= 4096:
       return min(current_best_size, 8192)
   if time_left > 600:
       return 4096
   return 2048
```

### Let's review mathematical objective: We normalize sum1. Sequence values sum 1. Evaluator sum_a=1, so exact score=2n*maxconv. Our fast_score returns same. If we output sum1, OK. If we upsample, sum normalized. If final exact score maybe slightly different due FFT; exact final chooses.

### Potential Mistake in `gA` gradient for L-BFGS: We set gA = 2 β n conv(a_rev,p). But conv(a_rev,p) index i = ∑_{j} a_rev_j p_{i-j}? We earlier derived g_i = (B*W)[i+n-1] with B reversed a, W=p. Let's verify again with numpy convolution definition. `np.convolve(v,w)` full: c[k]=∑_i v[i] w[k-i]. If v=a_rev of length n: v[l]=a[n-1-l]. We take conv[l] index m=i+n-1. c[m]=∑_{l=0}^{n-1} a_rev[l] p[m-l] =∑_l a[n-1-l] p[i+n-1-l]. Let j=n-1-l => l=n-1-j. Then c=∑_{j=0}^{n-1} a[j] p[i+j]. Good. This is our q_i without 2. Good. So conv(a[::-1], p)[n-1:n-1+n] correct.

### In subgradient q scaling, q = n * conv. If p sum1, sum q_i = n ∑_i ∑_j a_j p_{i+j} = n ∑_j a_j * (#sum_i p_{i+j}). Since p length full, ∑ over i all? =n∑_j a_j=sum=1? Wait p sum over all k, but i restricted 0..n-1, so for each j, i such that i+j in [0,2n-2] all i 0..n-1 valid? yes j 0..n-1, i+j up to2n-2, all valid; p sum over all k, so ∑_i p_{i+j} is not total sum (it's sum of p over range j..j+n-1). So sum q not exactly n. Average maybe? But scale by percentile okay.

### L-BFGS gradient `a[free_idx]*(gA[free_idx]-s)` with gA=2βn conv. s=∑a gA. If gA large for ref high, gradient for free could be negative. Good.

### Is val in L-BFGS using d=n*c or c? Max score=2 max d. Minimizing logsumexp of d equivalent to max d. Scale by n means d values O(1). Good. If using c values ~1/n, beta would need huge. Good.

### In subgradient, d=n*c, max target=score/2. For uniform d_max~1. Good.

### LP with f sum1 uses M=max conv = max d / n. If we build A and b_ub=M. f feasible: f*b? We use M= max conv. f*f max M. Constraint f*b ≤M. Good. If M small ~1/n, b sums maybe. Fine. Potential numerical: Linprog b_ub tiny 1e-4. HiGHS should handle. Could scale variables by n to improve conditioning: Let y = n*b? Since b sum1, y sum n. Constraint f*y ≤ M*n. Objective sum y. M*n~1. y values O(n). That may be better. Let's adjust LP: Set variable y = n * b. Then f*y = n f*b ≤ n M. Max sum y. Starting f corresponds y=n f with sum n. After solving, b=y/n, normalize. Constraint matrix A same, b_ub = n*M. This avoids tiny b_ub. Let's implement. `b_ub = n*M*(1+1e-8)`. The solution y may have large values n. Objective c=-ones. Good. `g=res.x`; sum_y maybe ≥n; b=g/sum? Wait if y = n b, then to normalize b sum1, b = g / sum(g)? Since sum_y = n * sum_b. If not exactly feasible with sum? We can compute b=g / np.sum(g) (same as normalize); no need n. The objective maximizing sum_y. Feasible f: y=n*f, f*y? Actually constraints: f*(y/n) ≤ M => f*y ≤ nM. Good. So set b_ub = n*M. Solve max sum y. Then b = g/sum(g) (since scaling removes n). Good. Use this.

LP update `g` normalized sum1. Feasible y for f has sum n, but res.x may sum not exactly n if constraints? It is max sum. Good.

```
    if budget_s is None: budget_s=2400
    try: budget_s=float(budget_s)
    except: budget_s=2400

    # LP small

    if parent is not None? no.
    if budget_s > 1000 and time_left > 180:
       total_lp=min(200.0, budget_s*0.08, time_left*0.2)
       n_lp=256

       ranked_lp=rank_starts(starts_lp,3)
       if ranked_lp:

             if time.monotonic()>deadline: break
             ar=run_lp_refine(a, max(5.0,min(90.0,per)))

    reserve=0.0
    if n_main < 4096 and time_left > 500: reserve=250.0

    if ranked_main:
       total_main=max(30.0, main_deadline-time.monotonic())
       per_start=max(30.0, min(280.0, total_main/float(len(ranked_main))))
       for idx,(sc0,a0) in enumerate(ranked_main):
          if time.monotonic() >= main_deadline: break
          remaining=main_deadline-time.monotonic()

          if per < 20: per=remaining-2.0

       else: large=best_hist.copy()
       sc=fast_score(large); add_history(history,large,sc); print
       if time.monotonic() < deadline - 150:
          budget=deadline-time.monotonic()-120.0
          if budget > 60:
             if n_final <= 4096:
                large2=run_refine(large,budget,rng,allow_lbfgs=True)

          polished=exact_local_refine(seq, min(90.0, time_left-20.0), rng)

    # final exact
    if not history:
       a=np.ones(1024,dtype=float); return a.tolist()
    history.sort(key=operator.itemgetter(0))
    best_seq=history[0][1]
    best_exact=exact_score_array(best_seq)
    for sc,seq in history[:14]:
       if seq.size > 10000: continue
       es=exact_score_array(seq)
       if math.isfinite(es) and es < best_exact:
          best_exact=es; best_seq=seq.copy()
    final=sanitize_sequence(best_seq)
    # If final sum <0.01? normalize
    final=normalize_sequence(final)
    return [float(x) for x in final]
```

```python
"""
Search for non-negative step-function heights minimizing
    2*n*max(a*a) / sum(a)^2.

The code uses FFT-based scoring for many fast evaluations, L-BFGS on a
smoothed max-convolution objective, multiplicative-gradient descent,
optional small-scale LP polishing from the projection idea in the prompt,
and final exact-evaluator polishing.  It returns the best sequence found.
"""

import time
import math
import operator

import numpy as np
import scipy.fft as sfft
import scipy.optimize as sopt


def get_parent_sequence():
    """Return the global parent sequence if present, sanitized."""
    try:
        p = height_sequence_1
    except NameError:
        return None
    p = sanitize_sequence(p)
    if p.size == 0:
        return None
    return p


def sanitize_sequence(a):
    """Convert input to a 1D non-negative finite float array."""
    if a is None:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    out = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if out.size == 0:
        return out
    out = np.where(np.isfinite(out), out, 0.0)
    out = np.maximum(out, 0.0)
    out = np.minimum(out, 1000.0)
    return out


def normalize_sequence(a, target=1.0):
    """Normalize a non-negative sequence to a fixed positive sum."""
    a = sanitize_sequence(a)
    if a.size == 0:
        return a
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(a.size, float(target) / float(a.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return a / s * float(target)


def convolve_fft(a, b=None):
    """Fast full linear convolution of non-negative sequences."""
    a = sanitize_sequence(a)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)

    if b is None:
        n = a.size
        L = int(sfft.next_fast_len(2 * n - 1))
        fa = sfft.rfft(a, n=L)
        y = sfft.irfft(fa * fa, n=L)
        out = y[:2 * n - 1]
    else:
        b = sanitize_sequence(b)
        if b.size == 0:
            return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
        n = a.size
        m = b.size
        L = int(sfft.next_fast_len(n + m - 1))
        fa = sfft.rfft(a, n=L)
        fb = sfft.rfft(b, n=L)
        y = sfft.irfft(fa * fb, n=L)
        out = y[:n + m - 1]

    out = np.asarray(out, dtype=np.float64)
    out = np.where(out < 0.0, 0.0, out)
    return out


def fast_score(a):
    """FFT approximation of evaluate_sequence. Faster for searching."""
    a = sanitize_sequence(a)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s <= 1e-12 or not math.isfinite(s):
        return float("inf")
    c = convolve_fft(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    mx = float(np.max(c))
    if not math.isfinite(mx):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * max(0.0, mx) / (s * s))


def exact_score_array(a):
    """Exact evaluation if practical; otherwise falls back to FFT score."""
    a = sanitize_sequence(a)
    if a.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    if a.size > 12000:
        return fast_score(a)
    try:
        val = evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in a])
    except Exception:
        val = float("inf")
    if not math.isfinite(val):
        return float("inf")
    return float(val)


def add_history(history, a, score=None, max_items=28):
    """Keep a small list of top candidates by FFT score."""
    a = sanitize_sequence(a)
    if a.size == 0:
        return
    if score is None or not math.isfinite(float(score)):
        score = fast_score(a)
    score = float(score)
    if not math.isfinite(score):
        return
    history.append((score, a.astype(np.float64, copy=True)))
    history.sort(key=operator.itemgetter(0))
    if len(history) > max_items:
        del history[-1]


def interpolate_sequence(a, n):
    """Resample a sequence to length n and normalize."""
    a = normalize_sequence(a)
    n = int(max(1, n))
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    if a.size == 1:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size, dtype=np.float64)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    b = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    return normalize_sequence(b)


def rank_starts(starts, top_k=10):
    """Rank starts by FFT score and return the best top_k."""
    ranked = []
    for a in starts:
        a = normalize_sequence(a)
        if a.size == 0:
            continue
        sc = fast_score(a)
        if math.isfinite(sc):
            ranked.append((sc, a))
    ranked.sort(key=operator.itemgetter(0))
    if top_k > 0 and len(ranked) > top_k:
        ranked = ranked[:top_k]
    return ranked


def make_starts(n, parent, rng, max_starts=80):
    """Generate diverse non-negative starting sequences."""
    n = int(max(1, n))
    starts = []

    starts.append(normalize_sequence(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)))

    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)

    for alpha in (-8.0, -5.0, -3.0, -1.5, -0.7, 0.7, 1.5, 3.0, 5.0, 8.0):
        z = np.clip(alpha * x, -25.0, 25.0)
        starts.append(normalize_sequence(np.exp(z)))

    for p in (0.4, 0.7, 1.0, 1.7, 2.5, 4.0):
        a1 = np.power(np.maximum(1.0 - 0.99 * x, 1e-6), p)
        a2 = np.power(np.maximum(0.01 + x, 1e-6), p)
        starts.append(normalize_sequence(a1 + 1e-8))
        starts.append(normalize_sequence(a2 + 1e-8))

    t = x - 0.5
    for A in (-2.0, -1.5, -1.0, -0.6, -0.2, 0.2, 0.6, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0):
        for B in (-1.0, -0.5, 0.0, 0.5, 1.0):
            z = np.clip(2.0 * A * t + 3.0 * B * (t * t), -12.0, 12.0)
            starts.append(normalize_sequence(np.exp(z)))

    for _ in range(12):
        r = rng.standard_normal(n)
        r = np.cumsum(r)
        r = r - np.linspace(r[0], r[-1], n)
        r = np.cumsum(r)
        r = r - np.linspace(r[0], r[-1], n)
        z = np.clip(0.6 * r, -15.0, 15.0)
        starts.append(normalize_sequence(np.exp(z)))

    for _ in range(16):
        deg = int(rng.integers(3, 9))
        coeff = rng.normal(size=deg + 1, scale=1.4)
        z = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        power = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for c in coeff:
            z += c * power
            power = power * t
        starts.append(normalize_sequence(np.exp(np.clip(z, -12.0, 12.0))))

    if parent is not None and parent.size >= 2:
        starts.append(interpolate_sequence(parent, n))
        starts.append(interpolate_sequence(parent[::-1], n))

    if len(starts) > max_starts:
        del starts[max_starts:]
    return starts


class _SmoothObjective:
    """Smoothed max convolution objective with analytic gradient."""

    def __init__(self, n, beta, free_idx, ref):
        self.n = int(n)
        self.beta = float(beta)
        self.free_idx = np.asarray(free_idx, dtype=int)
        self.ref = int(ref)

    def __call__(self, x):
        n = self.n
        free_idx = self.free_idx
        ref = self.ref
        beta = self.beta

        x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
        if x.size != free_idx.size:
            return 1.0e100, np.zeros(free_idx.size, dtype=np.float64)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
            return 1.0e100, np.zeros(free_idx.size, dtype=np.float64)

        x_full = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        x_full[free_idx] = x
        x_full[ref] = 0.0

        z = np.clip(x_full, -25.0, 25.0)
        a = np.exp(z)
        s = float(np.sum(a))
        if s <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(s):
            return 1.0e100, np.zeros(free_idx.size, dtype=np.float64)
        a = a / s

        c = convolve_fft(a)
        if c.size == 0:
            return 1.0e100, np.zeros(free_idx.size, dtype=np.float64)
        d = float(n) * c

        m = float(np.max(d))
        z2 = beta * (d - m)
        z2 = np.clip(z2, -700.0, 0.0)
        e = np.exp(z2)
        sume = float(np.sum(e))
        if sume <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(sume):
            return 1.0e100, np.zeros(free_idx.size, dtype=np.float64)

        p = e / sume
        val = beta * m + math.log(sume)
        if not math.isfinite(val):
            return 1.0e100, np.zeros(free_idx.size, dtype=np.float64)

        gvec = convolve_fft(a[::-1], p)
        if gvec.size < max(1, 2 * n - 1):
            return 1.0e100, np.zeros(free_idx.size, dtype=np.float64)

        grad_full = gvec[n - 1:n - 1 + n]
        if grad_full.size != n:
            return 1.0e100, np.zeros(free_idx.size, dtype=np.float64)

        gA = (2.0 * beta * float(n)) * grad_full
        gA = np.maximum(gA, 0.0)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(gA)):
            return 1.0e100, np.zeros(free_idx.size, dtype=np.float64)

        sprod = float(np.dot(a, gA))
        if not math.isfinite(sprod):
            return 1.0e100, np.zeros(free_idx.size, dtype=np.float64)

        grad = a[free_idx] * (gA[free_idx] - sprod)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
            return 1.0e100, np.zeros(free_idx.size, dtype=np.float64)

        return float(val), grad.astype(np.float64, copy=False)


def run_lbfgs(a, time_budget, betas=(2.0, 5.0, 12.0)):
    """Run L-BFGS on the smoothed objective from a starting sequence."""
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    a = normalize_sequence(a)
    n = a.size
    if n < 2 or time_budget < 20.0:
        return a

    ref = int(np.argmax(a))
    free_mask = np.ones(n, dtype=bool)
    free_mask[ref] = False
    free_idx = np.nonzero(free_mask)[0]

    a_ref = max(float(a[ref]), 1e-300)
    x_full = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300) / a_ref)
    x_full[ref] = 0.0
    x_full = np.clip(x_full, -25.0, 25.0)
    x = x_full[free_idx].astype(np.float64, copy=True)

    best = a.copy()
    best_score = fast_score(a)

    if n <= 1024:
        maxfun = 350
        maxiter = 140
    elif n <= 2048:
        maxfun = 250
        maxiter = 100
    elif n <= 4096:
        maxfun = 160
        maxiter = 70
    else:
        maxfun = 90
        maxiter = 45

    beta_list = list(betas)
    if n > 3500 and len(beta_list) > 2:
        beta_list = beta_list[:2]

    for beta in beta_list:
        if time.monotonic() - t0 >= max(0.0, time_budget - 1.0):
            break
        obj = _SmoothObjective(n, float(beta), free_idx, ref)
        x_new = x.copy()
        try:
            res = sopt.minimize(
                obj,
                x_new,
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                options={"maxiter": maxiter, "maxfun": maxfun,
                         "ftol": 1e-12, "gtol": 1e-8, "disp": False}
            )
            if res is not None and getattr(res, "x", None) is not None:
                rx = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
                if rx.size == free_idx.size and np.all(np.isfinite(rx)):
                    x_new = rx
        except Exception:
            x_new = x.copy()

        if x_new.size != free_idx.size or not np.all(np.isfinite(x_new)):
            x_new = x.copy()

        x_full_new = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        x_full_new[free_idx] = x_new
        x_full_new[ref] = 0.0
        z = np.clip(x_full_new, -25.0, 25.0)
        arr = np.exp(z)
        s = float(np.sum(arr))
        if s > 0.0 and math.isfinite(s):
            arr = arr / s
            sc = fast_score(arr)
            if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best = arr
                best_score = sc
                x = x_new.copy()

    return best


def run_subgradient(a, time_budget, rng):
    """Monotone multiplicative subgradient / active-lag smoothing."""
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    a = normalize_sequence(a)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0 or time_budget <= 0.0:
        return a

    best = a.copy()
    best_score = fast_score(a)
    current = a.copy()
    current_score = best_score

    temp_factor = 0.22
    stall = 0
    etas = (0.02, 0.05, 0.1, 0.2, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 4.0)

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < max(0.0, time_budget - 0.5):
        c = convolve_fft(current)
        if c.size == 0:
            break
        d = float(n) * c
        maxd = float(np.max(d))
        meand = float(np.mean(d))
        if maxd <= meand + 1e-12:
            break

        temp = (maxd - meand) * temp_factor
        if temp < 1e-4:
            temp = 1e-3

        z = (d - maxd) / temp
        z = np.clip(z, -700.0, 0.0)
        w = np.exp(z)
        w += 1e-7
        sw = float(np.sum(w))
        if sw <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(sw):
            break
        w = w / sw

        g = convolve_fft(current[::-1], w)
        if g.size < max(1, 2 * n - 1):
            break
        q = g[n - 1:n - 1 + n]
        if q.size != n:
            break

        q = np.maximum(q, 0.0)
        q = q * float(n)
        p95 = float(np.percentile(q, 95))
        if p95 <= 1e-12 or not math.isfinite(p95):
            p95 = 1.0
        qn = np.clip(q / p95, -10.0, 10.0)

        step_best_score = current_score
        step_best = current
        improved = False

        for eta in etas:
            cand = current * np.exp(-eta * qn)
            cand = np.maximum(cand, 1e-300)
            scand = float(np.sum(cand))
            if scand <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(scand):
                continue
            cand = cand / scand
            sc = fast_score(cand)
            if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < step_best_score - 1e-12:
                step_best_score = sc
                step_best = cand
                improved = True

        if improved:
            current = step_best
            current_score = step_best_score
            if current_score < best_score:
                best = current.copy()
                best_score = current_score
            stall = 0
            temp_factor = max(0.05, temp_factor * 0.985)
        else:
            stall += 1
            temp_factor = min(1.2, temp_factor * 1.5)

            if stall >= 3:
                base = best if stall >= 5 else current
                noise_level = 0.05 + 0.035 * abs(math.sin(0.71 * stall))
                vec = np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, noise_level, size=n))
                cand = normalize_sequence(base * vec)
                sc = fast_score(cand)
                if math.isfinite(sc):
                    if sc < best_score:
                        best = cand.copy()
                        best_score = sc
                        current = cand
                        current_score = sc
                        stall = 0
                    elif stall >= 5 and sc < best_score * 1.05 and sc < current_score * 1.25:
                        current = cand
                        current_score = sc

            if stall > 30:
                break

    return best


def run_lp_refine(a, time_budget):
    """Small-n LP polishing based on fixed-convolution constraints."""
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    a = normalize_sequence(a)
    n = a.size
    if n < 8 or n > 256 or time_budget < 5.0:
        return a

    best = a.copy()
    best_score = fast_score(a)
    c = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    bounds = np.zeros((n, 2), dtype=np.float64)
    bounds[:, 1] = np.inf

    stall = 0
    for _ in range(35):
        if time.monotonic() - t0 > max(0.0, time_budget - 1.0):
            break

        conv = convolve_fft(best)
        M = float(np.max(conv)) if conv.size else 0.0
        if M <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(M):
            break

        A = np.zeros((2 * n - 1, n), dtype=np.float64)
        for j in range(n):
            A[j:j + n, j] = best

        b_ub = np.full(2 * n - 1, float(n) * M * (1.0 + 1e-8), dtype=np.float64)

        try:
            res = sopt.linprog(
                c,
                A_ub=A,
                b_ub=b_ub,
                bounds=bounds,
                method="highs"
            )
        except Exception:
            break

        if res is None or getattr(res, "success", False) is False or getattr(res, "x", None) is None:
            break

        g = sanitize_sequence(np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64))
        sg = float(np.sum(g))
        if sg <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(sg):
            break
        g = g / sg

        improved = False
        for t in (0.1, 0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1.0):
            h = (1.0 - t) * best + t * g
            h = normalize_sequence(h)
            sc = fast_score(h)
            if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-10:
                best = h
                best_score = sc
                improved = True

        if improved:
            stall = 0
        else:
            stall += 1
            if stall > 6:
                break

    return best


def run_refine(a, time_budget, rng, allow_lbfgs=True):
    """Combined L-BFGS + subgradient polish."""
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    a = normalize_sequence(a)
    best = a.copy()
    best_score = fast_score(a)

    if allow_lbfgs and time_budget > 60.0:
        lbudget = min(180.0, 0.35 * time_budget)
        if lbudget > 25.0:
            al = run_lbfgs(a, lbudget, betas=(2.0, 5.0, 12.0))
            sc = fast_score(al)
            if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best = al
                best_score = sc

    sub_budget = time_budget - (time.monotonic() - t0) - 1.0
    if sub_budget > 10.0:
        asg = run_subgradient(best, sub_budget, rng)
        sc = fast_score(asg)
        if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best = asg
            best_score = sc

    return best


def exact_local_refine(a, time_budget, rng):
    """Exact evaluator hill climbing with small multiplicative block moves."""
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    a = normalize_sequence(a)
    best = a.copy()
    best_score = exact_score_array(a)
    if not math.isfinite(best_score):
        return a

    n = a.size
    if n <= 0:
        return a

    for _ in range(10000):
        if time.monotonic() - t0 > max(0.0, time_budget - 2.0):
            break

        width = max(1, int(n * (0.003 + 0.045 * rng.random())))
        if width >= n:
            width = max(1, n // 4)
        if n - width <= 0:
            start = 0
        else:
            start = int(rng.integers(0, n - width + 1))

        b = best.copy()
        sigma = 0.07 + 0.12 * rng.random()
        b[start:start + width] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma))
        b = normalize_sequence(b)

        sc = exact_score_array(b)
        if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
            best = b
            best_score = sc

    return best


def choose_final_n(parent, current_size, time_left):
    """Choose a final sequence length based on available time and parent."""
    current_size = int(max(1, current_size))
    if time_left < 320.0:
        return int(min(max(1024, current_size), 4096))

    if parent is not None and 3000 <= parent.size <= 8192:
        return int(parent.size)

    if current_size >= 4096:
        return int(min(current_size, 8192))

    if time_left > 750.0:
        return 4096
    return 2048


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """
    Main search entry point.

    Uses a staged strategy:
      1. optional small LP polishing to generate a strong low-resolution shape,
      2. medium-resolution L-BFGS + subgradient refinement from many starts,
      3. upscale and polish to a final practical length,
      4. exact-evaluator local polish and final exact selection.
    """
    if budget_s is None:
        budget_s = 2400.0
    try:
        budget_s = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget_s = 2400.0

    t_start = time.monotonic()
    hard_deadline = t_start + max(0.0, budget_s - 3.0)
    deadline = t_start + max(0.0, budget_s - 15.0)

    try:
        seed_int = int(seed)
    except Exception:
        seed_int = 42
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed_int)

    parent = get_parent_sequence()
    history = []

    if parent is not None:
        ps = sanitize_sequence(parent)
        pfs = fast_score(ps)
        if math.isfinite(pfs):
            add_history(history, ps, pfs)
            pes = exact_score_array(ps)
            print(f"parent len={ps.size} fast={pfs:.8f} exact={pes:.8f}", flush=True)

    time_left = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if budget_s > 1000.0 and time_left > 180.0:
        total_lp = min(150.0, 0.07 * budget_s, 0.25 * time_left)
        if total_lp > 40.0:
            n_lp = 192
            starts_lp = make_starts(n_lp, parent, rng, max_starts=14)
            ranked_lp = rank_starts(starts_lp, 3)
            if ranked_lp:
                per = total_lp / float(len(ranked_lp))
                for sc0, a0 in ranked_lp:
                    if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                        break
                    per_call = min(90.0, max(5.0, per))
                    al = run_lp_refine(a0, per_call)
                    sc = fast_score(al)
                    add_history(history, al, sc)
                    print(f"LP small best fast={sc:.8f} len={al.size}", flush=True)

    if budget_s < 1200.0:
        n_main = 1024
    else:
        n_main = 2048

    starts_main = make_starts(n_main, parent, rng, max_starts=84)
    if history:
        bh = history[0][1]
        if bh.size == n_main:
            starts_main.append(bh.copy())
        else:
            starts_main.append(interpolate_sequence(bh, n_main))

    ranked_main = rank_starts(starts_main, 8)

    reserve = 0.0
    if n_main < 4096 and deadline - time.monotonic() > 520.0:
        reserve = 260.0
    main_deadline = deadline - reserve

    if ranked_main:
        total_main = min(max(30.0, main_deadline - time.monotonic()), 1800.0)
        per_start = max(30.0, min(290.0, total_main / float(len(ranked_main))))
        for idx, (sc0, a0) in enumerate(ranked_main):
            if time.monotonic() >= main_deadline:
                break
            remaining = main_deadline - time.monotonic()
            if remaining <= 10.0:
                break
            per = min(per_start, remaining - 2.0)
            if per <= 8.0:
                break
            a1 = run_refine(a0, per, rng, allow_lbfgs=(idx < 5 and per > 80.0))
            s1 = fast_score(a1)
            add_history(history, a1, s1)
            print(
                f"main {idx+1}/{len(ranked_main)} start={sc0:.8f} "
                f"best_fast={s1:.8f} len={a1.size} "
                f"hist_top={history[0][0] if history else float('inf'):.8f}",
                flush=True
            )

    if history:
        time_left = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if time_left > 270.0:
            best_hist = history[0][1]
            n_final = choose_final_n(parent, best_hist.size, time_left)
            if n_final == best_hist.size:
                large = best_hist.copy()
            else:
                large = interpolate_sequence(best_hist, n_final)
            sc = fast_score(large)
            add_history(history, large, sc)
            print(f"upsampled len={large.size} fast={sc:.8f}", flush=True)

            budget_final = deadline - time.monotonic() - 120.0
            if budget_final > 70.0:
                if n_final <= 4096:
                    large2 = run_refine(large, budget_final, rng, allow_lbfgs=True)
                else:
                    large2 = run_subgradient(large, budget_final, rng)
                s2 = fast_score(large2)
                add_history(history, large2, s2)
                print(f"final refined len={large2.size} fast={s2:.8f}", flush=True)

    time_left = hard_deadline - time.monotonic()
    if history and time_left > 120.0:
        seq = history[0][1]
        if seq.size <= 8000:
            local_budget = min(80.0, time_left - 70.0)
            polished = exact_local_refine(seq, local_budget, rng)
            pfs = fast_score(polished)
            pes = exact_score_array(polished)
            add_history(history, polished, pfs)
            print(f"exact local len={polished.size} fast={pfs:.8f} exact={pes:.8f}", flush=True)

    if not history:
        a = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64)
        return [float(x) for x in a]

    history.sort(key=operator.itemgetter(0))
    best_seq = history[0][1]
    best_exact = exact_score_array(best_seq)

    remaining_exact = hard_deadline - time.monotonic() - 1.0
    if remaining_exact > 1.0:
        exact_limit = min(70.0, max(5.0, remaining_exact))
        exact_deadline = time.monotonic() + exact_limit
        for sc, seq in history[:16]:
            if time.monotonic() >= exact_deadline:
                break
            if seq.size <= 0 or seq.size > 10000:
                continue
            es = exact_score_array(seq)
            if math.isfinite(es) and es < best_exact:
                best_exact = es
                best_seq = seq.copy()

    final = sanitize_sequence(best_seq)
    final = normalize_sequence(final)

    fsc = fast_score(final)
    es = exact_score_array(final)
    print(f"FINAL len={final.size} fast={fsc:.8f} exact={es:.8f}", flush=True)

    return [float(x) for x in final]
```
